# IMPERIA COLD WAR — France 1970 bilateral diplomacy.


COLDWAR_FRA2_accept_pompidou_europe = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe
			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_pompidou_europe = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_pompidou_europe
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_pompidou_europe set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_party_pompidou_europe clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_pompidou_europe clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0055 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		GER = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0055 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		FRA = {
		57 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		GER = {
		42 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_preview_pompidou_europe = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_france_accept_heading
	FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0055 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_france
		FRA = {
			57 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_pompidou_europe_recipient_heading
	GER = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0055 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_GER
		GER = {
			42 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_pompidou_europe = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_pompidou_europe set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_pompidou_europe = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_pompidou_europe western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_party_pompidou_europe } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_pompidou_europe } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_pompidou_europe = yes
	}
}



COLDWAR_FRA2_accept_atlantic_liberal = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal
			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_atlantic_liberal = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_atlantic_liberal
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_atlantic_liberal set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_party_atlantic_liberal clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_atlantic_liberal clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_liberal clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_liberal
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0065 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		USA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0045 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		USA = {
		811 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = offices
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_france_research_1 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
		USA = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_recipient_research_2 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 }
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_preview_atlantic_liberal = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_france_accept_heading
	FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0065 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_atlantic_liberal_france
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_france_research_1 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_recipient_heading
	USA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0045 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_atlantic_liberal_USA
		USA = {
			811 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_recipient_research_2 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_atlantic_liberal = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_liberal value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_atlantic_liberal set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_liberal value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_atlantic_liberal = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal western_liberals_are_in_power = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_liberal } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_party_atlantic_liberal } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_atlantic_liberal } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_atlantic_liberal = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_accept_atlantic_security = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_security
			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_security COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_atlantic_security = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_atlantic_security
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_atlantic_security set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_party_atlantic_security clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_atlantic_security clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_security clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_security }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_security
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_security
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0045 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		ENG = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0045 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		FRA = {
		navy_experience = 15
		add_doctrine_cost_reduction = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_security_france_doctrine_2 category = naval_doctrine cost_reduction = 0.25 uses = 1 }
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_security_france_research_3 category = CAT_naval bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
		ENG = {
		navy_experience = 15
		add_doctrine_cost_reduction = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_security_recipient_doctrine_2 category = naval_doctrine cost_reduction = 0.25 uses = 1 }
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_security_recipient_research_3 category = CAT_naval bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_accept_rhine = {
	if = {

			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_rhine COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_rhine = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_rhine
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_rhine set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_regional_industry clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_regional_industry clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_rhine clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_rhine }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_rhine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_rhine
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.011 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		GER = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.011 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		FRA = {
		57 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		GER = {
		42 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_rhine_france_research_2 category = CAT_industry bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
		GER = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_rhine_recipient_research_2 category = CAT_industry bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_fp_saudi_civil = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_saudi_civil FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_saudi_civil } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_saudi_civil
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_saudi_civil value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_saudi_civil clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_2 set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_saudi_civil value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_fp_saudi_civil = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_saudi_civil  NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_saudi_civil } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_arab_contracts_2 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_2 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_saudi_civil } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_saudi_civil = yes
	}
}

		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_lebanon_field set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_arab_contracts_3 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_3 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_lebanon_field clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_lebanon_field }

		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_lebanon_field

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0065 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_fp_djibouti_defence = {

		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_djibouti_defence
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_djibouti_defence value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_djibouti_defence clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_global_partnerships_3 set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_djibouti_defence value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_fp_djibouti_defence = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_djibouti_defence  NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_djibouti_defence } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_global_partnerships_3 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_global_partnerships_3 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_djibouti_defence } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_djibouti_defence = yes
	}
}

				if = { limit = { country_exists = SAU } SAU = { COLDWAR_FRA2_clear_retired_bilateral_budget = yes update_money_dirty_variable = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_bilateral_budget_retired_20261101 } }
				if = { limit = { country_exists = SEN } SEN = { COLDWAR_FRA2_clear_retired_bilateral_budget = yes update_money_dirty_variable = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_bilateral_budget_retired_20261101 } }

	}
}
